329	329 │       <q-btn unelevated no-caps color="primary" icon="o_add" label="新建任务" @click="onNewTask" />
330	330 │       <q-btn flat no-caps icon="o_stop_circle" label="全部停止" :disable="activeRows.length === 0" @click="onStopAll" />
331	331 │     </div>
332	332 │ 
333	333 │     <!-- Main layout: list + right panel -->
334	334 │     <div class="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
335	335 │       <!-- Left: task list with tabs -->
336	336 │       <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
337	337 │         <TableToolbar
338	338 │           v-model:search-model-value="searchText"
339	339 │           search-placeholder="搜索任务名称..."
340	340 │         />
341	341 │ 
342	342 │         <q-tabs v-model="activeTab" dense inline-label class="q-px-4">
343	343 │           <q-tab name="active" label="活动任务" no-caps />
344	344 │           <q-tab name="history" label="历史记录" no-caps />
345	345 │         </q-tabs>
346	346 │ 
347	347 │         <!-- Active tab -->
348	348 │         <div v-show="activeTab === 'active'" class="flex-1">
349	349 │           <DataTable
350	350 │             :columns="taskColumns"
351	351 │             :rows="activeRows"
352	352 │             row-key="instanceId"
353	353 │             selection="single"
354	354 │             :selected="selectedActiveRow"
355	355 │             container-height="calc(100vh - 240px)"
356	356 │             @row-click="(_row: TaskTableRow) => onActiveRowClick(_row)"
357	357 │             @update:selected="onActiveSelectionChange"
358	358 │           >
359	359 │             <template #no-data>
360	360 │               <div class="text-center w-full q-pa-lg rw-text-label">暂无活动任务</div>
361	361 │             </template>
362	362 │ 
363	363 │             <template #body-cell-scheduleKind="props">
364	364 │               <q-td :props="props">
365	365 │                 <q-chip
366	366 │                   dense
367	367 │                   outline
368	368 │                   :color="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(props.row.scheduleKind).color"
369	369 │                   :label="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(props.row.scheduleKind).label"
370	370 │                   class="q-ma-none"
371	371 │                 />
372	372 │               </q-td>
373	373 │             </template>
374	374 │ 
375	375 │             <template #body-cell-status="props">
376	376 │               <q-td :props="props">
377	377 │                 <StatusBadge :status="props.row.displayStatus" :status-map="TASK_STATUS_MAP" />
378	378 │               </q-td>
379	379 │             </template>
380	380 │ 
381	381 │             <template #body-cell-progress="props">
382	382 │               <q-td :props="props">
383	383 │                 <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
384	384 │                   <q-linear-progress
385	385 │                     :value="props.row.progressPercent / 100"
386	386 │                     color="primary"
387	387 │                     size="4px"
388	388 │                     class="flex-1"
389	389 │                     rounded
390	390 │                   />
391	391 │                   <span class="rw-text-desc text-xs min-w-[32px] text-right">{{ props.row.progressLabel }}</span>
392	392 │                 </div>
393	393 │               </q-td>
394	394 │             </template>
395	395 │ 
396	396 │             <template #body-cell-_actions="props">
397	397 │               <q-td :props="props">
398	398 │                 <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
399	399 │                   <q-btn
400	400 │                     v-if="props.row.lifecycle === 'created'"
401	401 │                     flat round dense icon="o_play_arrow" size="sm" color="positive"
402	402 │                     :loading="isOperating(`start-${props.row.instanceId}`)"
403	403 │                     @click.stop="onStart(props.row.instanceId)"
404	404 │                   />
405	405 │                   <q-btn
406	406 │                     v-if="props.row.lifecycle === 'running'"
407	407 │                     flat round dense icon="o_pause" size="sm" color="warning"
408	408 │                     :loading="isOperating(`pause-${props.row.instanceId}`)"
409	409 │                     @click.stop="onPause(props.row.instanceId)"
410	410 │                   />
411	411 │                   <q-btn
412	412 │                     v-if="props.row.lifecycle === 'paused'"
413	413 │                     flat round dense icon="o_play_arrow" size="sm" color="primary"
414	414 │                     :loading="isOperating(`resume-${props.row.instanceId}`)"
415	415 │                     @click.stop="onResume(props.row.instanceId)"
416	416 │                   />
417	417 │                   <q-btn
418	418 │                     v-if="props.row.lifecycle === 'running' || props.row.lifecycle === 'paused'"
419	419 │                     flat round dense icon="o_stop" size="sm" color="negative"
420	420 │                     @click.stop="onStop(props.row.instanceId)"
421	421 │                   />
422	422 │                   <q-btn
423	423 │                     v-if="props.row.lifecycle === 'created'"
424	424 │                     flat round dense icon="o_edit" size="sm" color="primary"
425	425 │                     @click.stop="onEditTask()"
426	426 │                   />
427	427 │                   <q-btn
428	428 │                     v-if="isTerminal(props.row.lifecycle)"
429	429 │                     flat round dense icon="o_delete" size="sm" color="negative"
430	430 │                     @click.stop="onRemove(props.row.instanceId)"
431	431 │                   />
432	432 │                 </div>
433	433 │               </q-td>
434	434 │             </template>
435	435 │           </DataTable>
436	436 │         </div>
437	437 │ 
438	438 │         <!-- History tab -->
439	439 │         <div v-show="activeTab === 'history'" class="flex-1">
440	440 │           <DataTable
441	441 │             :columns="historyColumns"
442	442 │             :rows="historyRows"
443	443 │             row-key="instanceId"
444	444 │             selection="single"
445	445 │             :selected="selectedHistoryRow"
446	446 │             container-height="calc(100vh - 240px)"
447	447 │             @row-click="(_row: HistoryTableRow) => onHistoryRowClick(_row)"
448	448 │             @update:selected="onHistorySelectionChange"
449	449 │           >
450	450 │             <template #no-data>
451	451 │               <div class="text-center w-full q-pa-lg rw-text-label">暂无历史记录</div>
452	452 │             </template>
453	453 │ 
454	454 │             <template #body-cell-scheduleKind="props">
455	455 │               <q-td :props="props">
456	456 │                 <q-chip
457	457 │                   dense
458	458 │                   outline
459	459 │                   :color="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(props.row.scheduleKind).color"
460	460 │                   :label="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(props.row.scheduleKind).label"
461	461 │                   class="q-ma-none"
462	462 │                 />
463	463 │               </q-td>
464	464 │             </template>
465	465 │ 
466	466 │             <template #body-cell-result="props">
467	467 │               <q-td :props="props">
468	468 │                 <StatusBadge :status="props.row.lifecycle" :status-map="TASK_STATUS_MAP" />
469	469 │               </q-td>
470	470 │             </template>
471	471 │ 
472	472 │             <template #body-cell-elapsed="props">
473	473 │               <q-td :props="props">
474	474 │                 <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ formatElapsed(props.row.elapsedMs) }}</span>
475	475 │               </q-td>
476	476 │             </template>
477	477 │ 
478	478 │             <template #body-cell-finishedAt="props">
479	479 │               <q-td :props="props">
480	480 │                 <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ formatTime(props.row.finishedAt) }}</span>
481	481 │               </q-td>
482	482 │             </template>
483	483 │ 
484	484 │             <template #body-cell-_actions="props">
485	485 │               <q-td :props="props">
486	486 │                 <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
487	487 │                   <q-btn
488	488 │                     flat round dense icon="o_replay" size="sm" color="primary"
489	489 │                     :loading="isOperating(`retry-${props.row.instanceId}`)"
490	490 │                     @click.stop="onRetry(props.row.instanceId)"
491	491 │                   />
492	492 │                   <q-btn
493	493 │                     flat round dense icon="o_delete" size="sm" color="negative"
494	494 │                     @click.stop="onRemove(props.row.instanceId)"
495	495 │                   />
496	496 │                 </div>
497	497 │               </q-td>
498	498 │             </template>
499	499 │           </DataTable>
500	500 │         </div>
501	501 │       </div>
502	502 │ 
503	503 │       <!-- Right panel: 360px -->
504	504 │       <div class="w-[360px] flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto rw-divider-l">
505	505 │         <template v-if="selectedInstance">
506	506 │           <!-- Summary for created state -->
507	507 │           <template v-if="selectedInstance.lifecycle === 'created'">
508	508 │             <div class="p-4 rw-divider-b">
509	509 │               <div class="mb-3">
510	510 │                 <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">任务名称</span>
511	511 │                 <div class="rw-text-value">{{ selectedInstance.definitionRef.name }}</div>
512	512 │               </div>
513	513 │               <div class="mb-3">
514	514 │                 <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">调度类型</span>
515	515 │                 <div class="mt-1">
516	516 │                   <q-chip
517	517 │                     dense
518	518 │                     outline
519	519 │                     :color="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind).color"
520	520 │                     :label="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind).label"
521	521 │                   />
522	522 │                 </div>
523	523 │               </div>
524	524 │               <div>
525	525 │                 <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">步骤数</span>
526	526 │                 <div class="rw-text-value">{{ selectedInstance.definitionRef.steps.length }}</div>
527	527 │               </div>
528	528 │             </div>
529	529 │ 
530	530 │             <div class="p-4">
531	531 │               <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
532	532 │                 <q-btn
533	533 │                   unelevated no-caps color="primary"
534	534 │                   icon="o_edit" label="编辑"
535	535 │                   :loading="isOperating('o_edit')"
536	536 │                   @click="onEditTask"
537	537 │                 />
538	538 │                 <q-btn
539	539 │                   unelevated no-caps color="positive"
540	540 │                   icon="o_play_arrow" label="启动"
541	541 │                   :loading="isOperating(`start-${selectedInstance.instanceId}`)"
542	542 │                   @click="onStart(selectedInstance.instanceId)"
543	543 │                 />
544	544 │               </div>
545	545 │             </div>
546	546 │           </template>
547	547 │ 
548	548 │           <!-- Execution detail for running/paused -->
549	549 │           <template v-else-if="!isTerminal(selectedInstance.lifecycle)">
550	550 │             <div class="p-4">
551	551 │               <TaskExecutionDetail
552	552 │                 :instance="selectedInstance"
553	553 │                 :progress="selectedProgress"
554	554 │                 :display-status="selectedDisplayStatus"
555	555 │                 :status-info="selectedStatusInfo"
556	556 │                 @pause="selectedInstance && onPause(selectedInstance.instanceId)"
557	557 │                 @resume="selectedInstance && onResume(selectedInstance.instanceId)"
558	558 │                 @stop="selectedInstance && onStop(selectedInstance.instanceId)"
559	559 │               />
560	560 │             </div>
561	561 │           </template>
562	562 │ 
563	563 │           <!-- Terminal state summary -->
564	564 │           <template v-else>
565	565 │             <div class="p-4 rw-divider-b">
566	566 │               <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
567	567 │                 <StatusBadge :status="selectedDisplayStatus" :status-map="TASK_STATUS_MAP" />
568	568 │                 <span class="rw-text-value">{{ selectedInstance.definitionRef.name }}</span>
569	569 │               </div>
570	570 │               <div class="mb-2">
571	571 │                 <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">调度类型</span>
572	572 │                 <div class="mt-1">
573	573 │                   <q-chip
574	574 │                     dense
575	575 │                     outline
576	576 │                     :color="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind).color"
577	577 │                     :label="resolveScheduleKindDisplay(selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind).label"
578	578 │                   />
579	579 │                 </div>
580	580 │               </div>
581	581 │               <div v-if="selectedInstance.error" class="rw-text-error text-xs mt-2">
582	582 │                 {{ selectedInstance.error }}
583	583 │               </div>
584	584 │             </div>
585	585 │             <div class="p-4">
586	586 │               <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
587	587 │                 <q-btn
588	588 │                   flat no-caps icon="o_replay" label="重新执行" color="primary"
589	589 │                   :loading="isOperating(`retry-${selectedInstance.instanceId}`)"
590	590 │                   @click="onRetry(selectedInstance.instanceId)"
591	591 │                 />
592	592 │                 <q-btn
593	593 │                   flat no-caps icon="o_delete" label="删除" color="negative"
594	594 │                   @click="onRemove(selectedInstance.instanceId)"
595	595 │                 />
596	596 │               </div>
597	597 │             </div>
598	598 │           </template>
599	599 │         </template>
600	600 │ 
601	601 │         <!-- Empty state -->
602	602 │         <div v-else class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 rw-text-desc">
603	603 │           <q-icon name="o_assignment" size="48px" color="grey" class="q-mb-sm" />
604	604 │           <p>请选择一个任务</p>
605	605 │         </div>
606	606 │       </div>
607	607 │     </div>
608	608 │ 
609	609 │     <!-- Task editor dialog -->
610	610 │     <q-dialog v-model="editor.isEditing.value" @hide="onEditorHide">
611	611 │       <q-card class="rw-dialog-xl">
612	612 │         <q-card-section>
613	613 │           <div class="text-h6">{{ editor.editingInstanceId.value ? '编辑任务' : '新建任务' }}</div>
614	614 │         </q-card-section>
615	615 │ 
616	616 │         <q-card-section class="q-pt-none rw-dialog-scroll-body">
617	617 │           <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
618	618 │             <!-- Basic info -->
619	619 │             <q-input
620	620 │               v-model="editor.taskName.value"
621	621 │               dense outlined
622	622 │               label="任务名称"
623	623 │               :rules="[val => !!val || '请输入任务名称']"
624	624 │             />
625	625 │ 
626	626 │             <!-- Schedule type -->
627	627 │             <div>
628	628 │               <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">调度类型</span>
629	629 │               <q-select
630	630 │                 v-model="editor.scheduleKind.value"
631	631 │                 :options="SCHEDULE_KIND_OPTIONS"
632	632 │                 emit-value
633	633 │                 map-options
634	634 │                 outlined dense
635	635 │                 class="mt-1"
636	636 │               />
637	637 │             </div>
638	638 │ 
639	639 │             <!-- Timer config -->
640	640 │             <template v-if="editor.scheduleKind.value === 'o_timer'">
641	641 │               <q-input
642	642 │                 v-model.number="editor.timerIntervalMs.value"
643	643 │                 dense outlined
644	644 │                 type="number"
645	645 │                 label="间隔 (ms)"
646	646 │                 :rules="[val => val > 0 || '间隔必须大于0']"
647	647 │               />
648	648 │               <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
649	649 │                 <q-toggle v-model="editor.timerInfinite.value" label="无限循环" />
650	650 │                 <q-input
651	651 │                   v-if="!editor.timerInfinite.value"
652	652 │                   v-model.number="editor.timerIterations.value"
653	653 │                   dense outlined
654	654 │                   type="number"
655	655 │                   label="执行次数"
656	656 │                   :rules="[val => val > 0 || '次数必须大于0']"
657	657 │                   class="w-40"
658	658 │                 />
659	659 │               </div>
660	660 │             </template>
661	661 │ 
662	662 │             <!-- Event config -->
663	663 │             <template v-if="editor.scheduleKind.value === 'event'">
664	664 │               <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
665	665 │                 <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
666	666 │                   <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">触发条件</span>
667	667 │                   <q-btn flat dense no-caps icon="o_add" label="添加条件" size="sm" color="primary" @click="editor.addEventCondition()" />
668	668 │                 </div>
669	669 │                 <div
670	670 │                   v-for="(cond, ci) in editor.eventConditions.value"
671	671 │                   :key="ci"
672	672 │                   class="flex items-center gap-2"
673	673 │                 >
674	674 │                   <q-select
675	675 │                     :model-value="cond.operator"
676	676 │                     :options="['eq', 'neq', 'gt', 'lt', 'gte', 'lte', 'contains', 'change', 'any']"
677	677 │                     outlined dense
678	678 │                     emit-value
679	679 │                     class="w-24"
680	680 │                     @update:model-value="editor.updateEventCondition(ci, { ...cond, operator: $event as ConditionTerm['operator'] })"
681	681 │                   />
682	682 │                   <q-input
683	683 │                     :model-value="String(cond.threshold)"
684	684 │                     outlined dense
685	685 │                     placeholder="阈值"
686	686 │                     class="flex-1"
687	687 │                     @update:model-value="editor.updateEventCondition(ci, { ...cond, threshold: $event })"
688	688 │                   />
689	689 │                   <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_close" size="xs" color="negative" @click="editor.removeEventCondition(ci)" />
690	690 │                 </div>
691	691 │                 <div v-if="editor.eventConditions.value.length === 0" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">至少添加一条触发条件</div>
692	692 │               </div>
693	693 │             </template>
694	694 │ 
695	695 │             <q-separator />
696	696 │ 
697	697 │             <!-- Steps editor -->
698	698 │             <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
699	699 │               <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
700	700 │                 <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">步骤列表</span>
701	701 │                 <q-btn-dropdown flat dense no-caps label="添加步骤" size="sm" color="primary">
702	702 │                   <q-list>
703	703 │                     <q-item
704	704 │                       v-for="opt in ADD_STEP_OPTIONS"
705	705 │                       :key="opt.value"
706	706 │                       clickable
707	707 │                       dense
708	708 │                       v-close-popup
709	709 │                       @click="onAddStep(opt.value)"
710	710 │                     >
711	711 │                       <q-item-section>{{ opt.label }}</q-item-section>
712	712 │                     </q-item>
713	713 │                   </q-list>
714	714 │                 </q-btn-dropdown>
715	715 │               </div>
716	716 │ 
717	717 │               <q-expansion-item
718	718 │                 v-for="(step, si) in editor.steps.value"
719	719 │                 :key="step.id"
720	720 │                 dense
721	721 │                 switch-toggle-side
722	722 │                 :label="step.name ?? STEP_KIND_LABELS[step.kind].label"
723	723 │                 :caption="STEP_KIND_LABELS[step.kind].label"
724	724 │                 header-class="rw-text-value text-sm"
725	725 │               >
726	726 │                 <div class="q-pl-md flex flex-col gap-3">
727	727 │                   <!-- Send step -->
728	728 │                   <template v-if="step.kind === 'o_send'">
729	729 │                     <q-input
730	730 │                       :model-value="step.name"
731	731 │                       dense outlined
732	732 │                       label="步骤名称"
733	733 │                       @update:model-value="editor.updateStep(si, { ...step, name: $event ?? '' })"
734	734 │                     />
735	735 │                     <div>
736	736 │                       <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">帧格式</span>
737	737 │                       <q-select
738	738 │                         :model-value="step.config.frameId"
739	739 │                         :options="frameService.listFrames().map(f => ({ value: f.id, label: f.name }))"
740	740 │                         emit-value map-options
741	741 │                         outlined dense clearable
742	742 │                         class="mt-1"
743	743 │                         @update:model-value="updateSendStepField(si, 'frameId', $event ?? '')"
744	744 │                       />
745	745 │                     </div>
746	746 │                     <div>
747	747 │                       <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">发送目标</span>
748	748 │                       <SendTargetSelector
749	749 │                         :model-value="step.config.targetId"
750	750 │                         :connection-service="connectionService"
751	751 │                         class="mt-1"
752	752 │                         @update:model-value="updateSendStepField(si, 'targetId', $event ?? '')"
753	753 │                       />
754	754 │                     </div>
755	755 │                     <template v-if="step.config.frameId && getFrameFields(step.config.frameId).length > 0">
756	756 │                       <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">字段值</span>
757	757 │                       <FieldEditWidget
758	758 │                         :fields="getFrameFields(step.config.frameId)"
759	759 │                         :values="step.config.userFieldValues ?? {}"
760	760 │                         direction="o_send"
761	761 │                         @update:values="updateSendStepField(si, 'userFieldValues', $event)"
762	762 │                       />
763	763 │                     </template>
764	764 │                     <q-input
765	765 │                       :model-value="step.config.intervalAfterMs"
766	766 │                       dense outlined
767	767 │                       type="number"
768	768 │                       label="发送后延时 (ms)"
769	769 │                       @update:model-value="updateSendStepField(si, 'intervalAfterMs', Number($event) || undefined)"
770	770 │                     />
771	771 │                     <q-btn
772	772 │                       v-if="si > 0 && editor.steps.value[si - 1]?.kind === 'o_send'"
773	773 │                       flat dense no-caps
774	774 │                       label="复制上一步字段值"
775	775 │                       size="sm"
776	776 │                       color="primary"
777	777 │                       @click="editor.duplicateStepValuesFromPrevious(si)"
778	778 │                     />
779	779 │                   </template>
780	780 │ 
781	781 │                   <!-- Wait condition step -->
782	782 │                   <template v-if="step.kind === 'wait-condition'">
783	783 │                     <q-input
784	784 │                       :model-value="step.name"
785	785 │                       dense outlined
786	786 │                       label="步骤名称"
787	787 │                       @update:model-value="editor.updateStep(si, { ...step, name: $event ?? '' })"
788	788 │                     />
789	789 │                     <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
790	790 │                       <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
791	791 │                         <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">等待条件</span>
792	792 │                         <q-btn flat dense no-caps icon="o_add" label="添加" size="sm" color="primary" @click="addWaitConditionTerm(si)" />
793	793 │                       </div>
794	794 │                       <div
795	795 │                         v-for="(term, ti) in step.config.conditions"
796	796 │                         :key="ti"
797	797 │                         class="flex items-center gap-2"
798	798 │                       >
799	799 │                         <q-input
800	800 │                           :model-value="String(term.threshold)"
801	801 │                           outlined dense
802	802 │                           placeholder="阈值"
803	803 │                           class="flex-1"
804	804 │                           @update:model-value="updateWaitConditionTerm(si, ti, { ...term, threshold: $event })"
805	805 │                         />
806	806 │                         <q-btn flat round dense icon="o_close" size="xs" color="negative" @click="removeWaitConditionTerm(si, ti)" />
807	807 │                       </div>
808	808 │                       <div v-if="step.config.conditions.length === 0" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">至少添加一条条件</div>
809	809 │                     </div>
810	810 │                     <q-input
811	811 │                       :model-value="step.config.timeoutMs"
812	812 │                       dense outlined
813	813 │                       type="number"
814	814 │                       label="超时时间 (ms)"
815	815 │                       :rules="[val => !val || val > 0 || '超时时间必须大于0']"
816	816 │                       @update:model-value="updateWaitConditionConfig(si, { ...step.config, timeoutMs: Number($event) || undefined })"
817	817 │                     />
818	818 │                     <div>
819	819 │                       <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">超时策略</span>

 1 │ <script setup lang="ts">
 2 │ import { computed, shallowRef, watch } from 'vue';
 3 │ import type { ConnectionService, TransportTargetSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
 4 │ 
 5 │ const props = defineProps<{
 6 │   readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
 7 │   readonly modelValue: string | null;
 8 │   readonly disable?: boolean;
 9 │ }>();
10 │ 
11 │ const emit = defineEmits<{
12 │   'update:modelValue': [value: string | null];
13 │ }>();
14 │ 
15 │ const targets = shallowRef<TransportTargetSnapshot[]>([]);
16 │ const selectedTargetId = computed({
17 │   get: () => props.modelValue,
18 │   set: (val) => emit('update:modelValue', val),
19 │ });
20 │ 
21 │ function refreshTargets(): void {
22 │   try {
23 │     targets.value = props.connectionService.listTransportTargets();
24 │   } catch (err) {
25 │     console.warn('Failed to refresh targets', err);
26 │     targets.value = [];
27 │   }
28 │ }
29 │ 
30 │ // Refresh targets on mount and when connection state might change
31 │ watch(() => props.connectionService, () => {
32 │   refreshTargets();
33 │ }, { immediate: true });
34 │ 
35 │ const targetOptions = computed(() =>
36 │   targets.value.map((t) => ({
37 │     value: t.targetId,
38 │     label: `${t.label} (${t.kind})`,
39 │     disable: !t.available,
40 │   })),
41 │ );
42 │ </script>
43 │ 
44 │ <template>
45 │   <q-select
46 │     v-model="selectedTargetId"
47 │     :options="targetOptions"
48 │     :disable="disable"

52 │     map-options
53 │     clearable
54 │     placeholder="选择发送目标"
55 │     class="w-full"

18 │ function addOption(): void {
19 │   const next = [...props.options, { value: '', label: '' }];

 14 │   remove: [];
 15 │ }>();
 16 │ 
 17 │ function formatRoute(s: ConnectionSummary): string {
 18 │   if (s.kind === 'serial') return s.routeLabel;
 19 │   return s.routeLabel;
 20 │ }
 21 │ </script>
 22 │ 
 23 │ <template>
 24 │   <q-card flat bordered class="connection-card">
 25 │     <div class="connection-card__body gap-3 py-2 px-3">

 31 │       <div class="connection-card__actions gap-1">
 32 │         <q-btn

 36 │           color="primary"

 62 │         </q-btn>

 65 │     <q-expansion-item

 96 │     const id = crypto.randomUUID();

100 │       autoConnect: autoConnect.value || undefined,
101 │     };
102 │ 
103 │     let config: TransportConfig;

137 │         };

144 │   } finally {

147 │ }

 1 │ <script setup lang="ts" generic="T extends Record<string, unknown>">
 2 │ import { computed, ref } from 'vue';
 3 │ import type { QTableColumn, QTableProps } from 'quasar';
 4 │ 
 5 │ interface DataTableProps {
 6 │   columns: QTableColumn[];
 7 │   rows: readonly T[];
 8 │   rowKey: string;
 9 │   loading?: boolean;
10 │   selection?: 'single' | 'multiple' | 'none';
11 │   selected?: readonly T[];
12 │   virtualScrollItemSize?: number;
13 │   containerHeight?: string;

15 │ 
16 │ const props = withDefaults(defineProps<DataTableProps>(), {
17 │   loading: false,
18 │   selection: 'none',
19 │   selected: () => [],
20 │   virtualScrollItemSize: 48,
21 │   containerHeight: 'calc(100vh - 200px)',
22 │ });
23 │ 
24 │ const emit = defineEmits<{
25 │   'row-click': [row: T, index: number, event: MouseEvent];
26 │   'update:selected': [selected: T[]];
27 │ }>();
28 │ 
29 │ const tableRef = ref<QTableProps | null>(null);
30 │ 
31 │ const computedSelected = computed({
32 │   get: () => [...props.selected],
33 │   set: (val: T[]) => emit('update:selected', val),
34 │ });
35 │ 
36 │ const showSelection = computed(() => props.selection !== 'none');
37 │ </script>
38 │

41 │     ref="tableRef"
42 │     flat

46 │     :loading="loading"

52 │     :style="{ maxHeight: containerHeight }"

===
  1 │ <script setup lang="ts">
  2 │ import { computed } from 'vue';
  3 │ import type { FrameFieldDefinition } from '@/features/frame';
  4 │ import type { SendFieldValue } from '@/features/send';
  5 │ 
  6 │ // --- Types ---
  7 │ 
  8 │ interface FieldSection {
  9 │   readonly key: string;
 10 │   readonly label: string;
 11 │   readonly color: string;
 12 │   readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
 13 │ }
 14 │ 
 15 │ // --- Props & Emits ---
 16 │ 
 17 │ const props = defineProps<{
 18 │   readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
 19 │   readonly values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
 20 │   readonly direction: 'send' | 'receive';
 21 │ }>();
 22 │ 
 23 │ const emit = defineEmits<{
 24 │   'update:values': [values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>];
 25 │ }>();
 26 │ 
 27 │ // --- Section definitions (O4: module-level constants) ---
 28 │ 
 29 │ const SECTION_DEFS = [
 30 │   { key: 'configurable', label: '可调字段', color: 'info' },
 31 │   { key: 'expression', label: '计算参数', color: 'warning' },
 32 │   { key: 'fixed', label: '不可配置', color: 'grey' },
 33 │ ] as const;
 34 │ 
 35 │ // --- Classification ---
 36 │ 
 37 │ function classifyField(field: FrameFieldDefinition): 'configurable' | 'expression' | 'fixed' {
 38 │   if (field.configurable) return 'configurable';
 39 │   if (field.expressionConfig) return 'expression';
 40 │   return 'fixed';
 41 │ }
 42 │ 
 43 │ const sections = computed<readonly FieldSection[]>(() => {
 44 │   const buckets = new Map<string, FrameFieldDefinition[]>();
 45 │   for (const def of SECTION_DEFS) {
 46 │     buckets.set(def.key, []);
 47 │   }
 48 │   for (const field of props.fields) {
 49 │     const bucket = classifyField(field);
 50 │     buckets.get(bucket)!.push(field);
 51 │   }
 52 │   return SECTION_DEFS
 53 │     .map((def) => ({
 54 │       key: def.key,
 55 │       label: def.label,
 56 │       color: def.color,
 57 │       fields: buckets.get(def.key)!,
 58 │     }))
 59 │     .filter((section) => section.fields.length > 0);
 60 │ });
 61 │ 
 62 │ // --- Helpers ---
 63 │ 
 64 │ const NUMERIC_DATA_TYPES = new Set([
 65 │   'uint8', 'int8', 'uint16', 'int16', 'uint32', 'int32',
 66 │   'uint64', 'int64', 'float', 'double',
 67 │ ]);
 68 │ 
 69 │ function isNumericField(field: FrameFieldDefinition): boolean {
 70 │   return NUMERIC_DATA_TYPES.has(field.dataType);
 71 │ }
 72 │ 
 73 │ function hasNonTrivialFactor(field: FrameFieldDefinition): boolean {
 74 │   return field.factor !== undefined && field.factor !== 1;
 75 │ }
 76 │ 
 77 │ function getDisplayValue(field: FrameFieldDefinition): string {
 78 │   const val = props.values[field.id];
 79 │   if (val !== undefined && val !== null) return String(val);
 80 │   return '--';
 81 │ }
 82 │ 
 83 │ function resolveFieldInputValue(field: FrameFieldDefinition): SendFieldValue {
 84 │   return props.values[field.id] ?? field.defaultValue ?? '';
 85 │ }
 86 │ 
 87 │ // --- Mutation ---
 88 │ 
 89 │ function onFieldChange(fieldId: string, value: SendFieldValue): void {
 90 │   emit('update:values', { ...props.values, [fieldId]: value });
 91 │ }
 92 │ 
 93 │ function onNumericInput(field: FrameFieldDefinition, raw: string | number | null): void {
 94 │   if (raw === null || raw === '') {
 95 │     onFieldChange(field.id, '');
 96 │     return;

  1 │ <script setup lang="ts">
  2 │ import { computed } from 'vue';
  3 │ import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress, TaskStepDefinition, TaskStepResult } from '@/features/task';
  4 │ import { STEP_KIND_LABELS } from '@/features/task/components/task-labels';
  5 │ import StatusBadge from './StatusBadge.vue';
  6 │ import { TASK_STATUS_MAP } from '@/features/task/components/taskStatusMap';
  7 │ import { isStepResultFailed } from '@/features/task';
  8 │ import { formatElapsed } from '@/shared/utils/format';
  9 │ 
 10 │ const props = defineProps<{
 11 │   readonly instance: TaskInstanceState;
 12 │   readonly progress: TaskProgress | null;
 13 │   readonly displayStatus: string;
 14 │   readonly statusInfo: { label: string; color: string };
 15 │ }>();
 16 │ 
 17 │ const emit = defineEmits<{
 18 │   pause: [];
 19 │   resume: [];
 20 │   stop: [];
 21 │ }>();
 22 │ 
 23 │ const stepStatuses = computed(() => {
 24 │   const steps = props.instance.definitionRef.steps;
 25 │   const results = props.instance.stepResults;
 26 │   const currentIter = props.instance.currentIteration;
 27 │   const currentStepIdx = props.instance.currentStepIndex;
 28 │ 
 29 │   const currentIterResults = results.filter((r) => r.iteration === currentIter);
 30 │   const resultMap = new Map<number, TaskStepResult>();
 31 │   for (const r of currentIterResults) {
 32 │     resultMap.set(r.stepIndex, r);
 33 │   }
 34 │ 
 35 │   return steps.map((step, idx) => {
 36 │     const result = resultMap.get(idx);
 37 │     let status: 'o_pending' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'skipped' = 'o_pending';
 38 │     if (result) {
 39 │       if (result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step') {
 40 │         status = 'skipped';
 41 │       } else if (isStepResultFailed(result)) {
 42 │         status = 'failed';
 43 │       } else {
 44 │         status = 'done';
 45 │       }
 46 │     } else if (idx === currentStepIdx) {
 47 │       status = 'running';
 48 │     }
 49 │     return { step, index: idx, status, result };
 50 │   });
 51 │ });
 52 │ 
 53 │ function formatStepResult(step: TaskStepDefinition, result: TaskStepResult | undefined): string {
 54 │   if (!result) return '';
 55 │   switch (step.kind) {
 56 │     case 'o_send':
 57 │       return result.kind === 'o_send'
 58 │         ? result.sendResult.kind === 'sent' ? '发送成功' : '发送失败'
 59 │         : '';
 60 │     case 'wait-condition':
 61 │       return result.kind === 'wait-condition'
 62 │         ? result.matched ? '条件匹配' : result.timedOut ? '超时' : ''
 63 │         : '';
 64 │     case 'delay':
 65 │       return result.kind === 'delay'
 66 │         ? result.completed ? '完成' : '中断'
 67 │         : '';
 68 │   }
 69 │ }
 70 │ 
 71 │ const isRunning = computed(() => props.instance.lifecycle === 'running');
 72 │ const isPaused = computed(() => props.instance.lifecycle === 'paused');
 73 │ 
 74 │ const progressPct = computed(() => {
 75 │   if (!props.progress) return 0;
 76 │   const total = props.progress.stepsTotal || 1;
 77 │   return Math.round((props.progress.stepsCompleted / total) * 100);
 78 │ });
 79 │ </script>
 80 │ 
 81 │ <template>
 82 │   <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
 83 │     <!-- Header -->
 84 │     <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
 85 │       <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
 86 │         <StatusBadge :status="displayStatus" :status-map="TASK_STATUS_MAP" />
 87 │         <span class="rw-text-value text-sm">{{ instance.definitionRef.name }}</span>
 88 │       </div>
 89 │     </div>
 90 │ 
 91 │     <!-- Progress -->
 92 │     <div v-if="progress" class="flex flex-col gap-1">
 93 │       <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
 94 │         <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">进度</span>
 95 │         <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ progress.stepsCompleted }}/{{ progress.stepsTotal }} ({{ progressPct }}%)</span>
 96 │       </div>
 97 │       <q-linear-progress :value="progressPct / 100" color="primary" size="6px" rounded />
 98 │     </div>
 99 │ 
100 │     <!-- Elapsed -->
101 │     <div v-if="progress" class="flex items-center justify-between">
102 │       <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">耗时</span>
103 │       <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ formatElapsed(progress.elapsedMs) }}</span>
104 │     </div>
105 │ 
106 │     <!-- Iteration info -->
107 │     <div v-if="progress && progress.iterationsTotal !== null" class="flex items-center justify-between">
108 │       <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">迭代</span>
109 │       <span class="rw-text-value text-xs">{{ progress.iterationsCompleted }}/{{ progress.iterationsTotal }}</span>
110 │     </div>
111 │ 
112 │     <!-- Step status list -->
113 │     <q-separator />
114 │     <div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
115 │       <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">步骤状态</span>
116 │       <div
117 │         v-for="item in stepStatuses"
118 │         :key="item.index"
119 │         class="flex items-center gap-2"
120 │       >
121 │         <q-icon
122 │           :name="item.status === 'running' ? 'o_pending' : item.status === 'done' ? 'o_check_circle' : item.status === 'failed' ? 'o_error' : item.status === 'skipped' ? 'o_skip_next' : 'o_radio_button_unchecked'"
123 │           :color="item.status === 'done' ? 'positive' : item.status === 'failed' ? 'negative' : item.status === 'running' ? 'primary' : 'grey'"
124 │           size="xs"
125 │         />
126 │         <span class="rw-text-value text-xs flex-1">
127 │           {{ item.step.name ?? STEP_KIND_LABELS[item.step.kind].label }} #{{ item.index + 1 }}
128 │         </span>
129 │         <span v-if="item.result" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
130 │           {{ formatStepResult(item.step, item.result) }}
131 │         </span>
132 │       </div>
133 │     </div>
134 │ 
135 │     <!-- Error -->
136 │     <div v-if="instance.error" class="rw-text-error text-xs p-2 rounded rw-surface-elevated">
137 │       {{ instance.error }}
138 │     </div>
139 │ 
140 │     <!-- Actions -->
141 │     <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
142 │       <q-btn
143 │         v-if="isRunning"
144 │         flat
145 │         no-caps
146 │         dense
147 │         icon="o_pause"
148 │         label="暂停"
149 │         color="warning"
150 │         size="sm"

152 │       />
153 │       <q-btn

# 前端 UI 审计报告

## 页面 1：Home `/`

### P1 表单
无表单。

### P2 弹窗
无弹窗。

### P3 工具栏
不适用（仪表盘页）。

### P4 表格
无表格。

### P5 布局/间距
- [P5-01] `@media` 断点内硬编码 `padding: 16px`，应改用 token 或 UnoCSS compact class | HomePage.vue:361
- [P5-02] 所有页面 scoped style 中 `min-height: 100%` 可用 UnoCSS `min-h-full` 替代，无需在 `<style>` 中声明 | HomePage.vue:221

### P6 状态展示
- [P6-01] 连接状态使用 `connectionStatusColor()` 函数手动映射 lifecycle → Quasar color，再传给 QBadge。应改用 StatusBadge + `statusMap` 统一模式（与 ConnectionCard 已用的 `connectionStatusMap` 重复逻辑） | HomePage.vue:119-126, 179-183
- [P6-02] 系统快照区域发送字节 `sendStats.totalBytesSent` 未格式化（大数无千分位） | HomePage.vue:205

### 其他
- [I-F8-01] 模板中无内联 `.filter()`/`.map()` — 通过 | 
- [I-CM2-01] 轮询使用 `usePolling()` — 通过 | HomePage.vue:113
- [I-O1-01] 状态声明未严格按 O1 分组（Service 引用和业务数据混杂，缺 `// =====` 分隔注释） | HomePage.vue:31-42

---

## 页面 2：Connection `/connection`

### P1 表单
无页面级表单。NewConnectionDialog 内表单审计见下方弹窗 section。

### P2 弹窗

**NewConnectionDialog**

- [P2-01] 使用 `:model-value` + `@update:model-value` 模式，非 `v-model` 双向绑定 — 违反 D1 | NewConnectionDialog.vue:158
- [P2-02] 串口表单的 `portPath` 字段无 `:rules` 校验（必填字段） | NewConnectionDialog.vue:217-224
- [P2-03] 弹窗内表单字段间距无统一 gap 容器，各字段间距靠 Quasar 默认 margin，不统一 | NewConnectionDialog.vue:205-320
- [P2-04] 类型选择页 footer "取消"按钮和表单页底部"取消/连接"按钮是两个独立区域，表单页 submit 按钮有 `:loading` — 通过 F4 | NewConnectionDialog.vue:311-318

### P3 工具栏
不适用（卡片列表页，无搜索/筛选）。

### P4 表格
无表格。

### P5 布局/间距
- [P5-03] `@media` 断点内硬编码 `padding: 16px` | ConnectionPage.vue:218
- [P5-04] scoped style 中声明 `min-height: 100%`，可用 UnoCSS `min-h-full` | ConnectionPage.vue:167

### P6 状态展示
- StatusBadge + connectionStatusMap 使用正确 — 通过 | ConnectionCard.vue:27

## 汇总

### 各 pattern 问题数量

| Pattern | 问题数 |
|---------|--------|
| P1 表单 | 9 |
| P2 弹窗 | 11 |
| P3 工具栏 | 0 |
| P4 表格 | 3 |
| P5 布局/间距 | 11 |
| P6 状态展示 | 5 |
| 其他（性能/CM/O） | 8 |
| **总计** | **47** |

### 跨页面共性问题（一次修复所有页面）

1. **Quasar spacing class 混用**：`q-mt-sm`/`q-mb-md`/`q-pa-lg` 等与 UnoCSS utility class 混用，应统一为 UnoCSS gap/mt/mb/px/py — 涉及几乎所有组件。最严重在 FrameFieldEditorDialog（12 处 `q-mt-sm`）。

2. **scoped style `min-height: 100%`**：6 个页面都有相同 pattern，可统一改用 UnoCSS `min-h-full` class。

3. **`@media` 断点硬编码 `padding: 16px`**：3 个页面（Home, Connection, CommandIngress）相同 pattern，应抽成 `p-page-compact` class。

4. **QForm 缺失**：SendPage 编辑弹窗、TaskManagePage 编辑器弹窗、CommandIngressPage 高亮弹窗均缺少 QForm 包裹。

5. **Dialog `:model-value` 替代 `v-model`**：NewConnectionDialog 和 ImportFrameDialog 两处。

6. **`$q.notify()` 替代 `useNotify()`**：FrameListPage 8 处直接调用。

### 页面特有问题

| 页面 | 特有问题 |
|------|---------|
| Home | connectionStatusColor 函数应改用 StatusBadge + statusMap |
| Connection | NewConnectionDialog portPath 缺必填校验 |
| Frame List | $q.notify 散落 8 处 |
| Frame Editor | FrameDetailPanel inline style `min-width: 80px`；大量 q-mt-sm |
| Send | 编辑弹窗缺 QForm |
| Task | 编辑器 300+ 行缺 QForm 和子组件拆分；v-show 双表格；模板内联 .map()×2 |
| Command Ingress | 高亮弹窗缺 @hide 清理和 :loading |

### 建议修复优先级

**P0 — 必须立即修复（功能缺陷）**

1. TaskManagePage 编辑器弹窗拆分子组件 + 加 QForm（300 行单组件不可维护）
2. SendPage 编辑弹窗加 QForm（:rules 不生效）
3. CommandIngressPage 高亮弹窗加 `@hide` 清理

**P1 — 应尽快修复（规范违规）**

4. 统一 FrameListPage `$q.notify` → `useNotify()`
5. NewConnectionDialog + ImportFrameDialog 改 `v-model`
6. FrameFieldEditorDialog 12 处 `q-mt-sm` → UnoCSS `mt-2` 或 flex `gap-2`
7. 所有页面 `min-height: 100%` → UnoCSS `min-h-full`
8. 3 页面 `padding: 16px` → token class
9. FrameDetailPanel `style="min-width: 80px"` → UnoCSS class

**P2 — 可计划修复（性能优化）**

10. TaskManagePage 双 tab `v-show` → `v-if`
11. TaskManagePage 模板内联 `.map()` × 2 → computed
12. TaskManagePage `resolveScheduleKindDisplay` 预计算到 row
